# rq6-threshold-dev

RQ6, the threshold control for weights 5 and 10 on development data: cross-entropy
scored at 0.079 and 0.041, the thresholds at which a calibrated cross-entropy model
reports what weights 5 and 10 report at 0.3 (t / (w(1 - t) + t), experiments/README.md,
Training), three seeds each. Weight 3 had its control (ce@0.125) in rq6-weight-dev;
weights 5 and 10 waited for a cache floor below 0.1 (systems.LOW_CACHE_FLOOR). Focal
loss is compared with ce@0.041 as well, as it sits beside weight 10. If a weight only
beats cross-entropy at 0.3, it has only moved the threshold. Weight 5's first seed was
trained on a CPU (karol), every other model on the Mac GPU.
